# InteropHub Local Site Map Catalog

Generated from `http://localhost:8080/hub/` after logging in as `nbunker@immregistries.org`.
Only `/hub` pages were recursively crawled. Links to other localhost apps and external sites were recorded but not followed.

## Crawl Summary

- Hub pages fetched: 219
- Route/page-type signatures represented: 109
- In-scope navigation edges: 4858
- Other localhost links documented but not crawled: 1
- External links documented but not crawled: 6

## Page Catalog

### `/hub/admin`
- Title: Admin Home - InteropHub
